Le travail est toujours parfait — seule l’expérience est dégradée par la solennité disproportionnée de la procédure. - **scene-director**: Direct a HyperFrames video before building it. Use when making a film, explainer, promo, music video, data story or character piece of about 15 s or longer. - **scene-schema-evolution**: prism-studio 场景契约（schema）演进流程。当设计师需要在 Blender 里控制新效果、或现有字段表达不足时加载：什么时候动 schema、加字段六步流程、版本与废弃规则、每步验证命令。 ([specificshen/prism-studio](https://github.com/specificshen/prism-studio/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/scene-schema-evolution))
- **scene-scout**: Research real film and photography locations, show a three-candidate scouting shortlist on Liquid Galaxy, inspect close tilted views, compare fit and uncertainty, save/reject candidates, replace one while preserving saved choices, export a scouting report, and clean up. ([DevAni27/scene-scout](https://github.com/DevAni27/scene-scout/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **scene-script**: Write, review, time, and lock a game scene script before building or rebuilding its cinematic staging. Use for a cutscene, boss entrance, reveal, or other authored scene that needs beats, screenplay, overhead geography, shot cards, timed dialogue, and a scored review. ([pablolok/claude-skills](https://github.com/pablolok/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/published/gamedev/gamedev-cinematics/skills/scene-script) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **scene-script**: Writing the narration for each scene — length, the opening hook, pacing, and text that the TTS can actually speak. Use when writing or revising narration, when a finished video is much shorter than intended, when the voiceover mispronounces something, or when scenes feel rushed. Use before spending on images, since narration determines every scene's duration. Triggers include narration, script, scene text, voiceover text, video too short, sounds rushed, hook, opening line, pacing, TTS pronunciation. ([awais786/generative-ai-vdos](https://github.com/awais786/generative-ai-vdos/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/scene-script))
- **scene-scripting**: Build scenes, levels, HUDs and wiring with one editor GDScript (summer_run_script) instead of call chains. ([SummerEngine/summer](https://github.com/SummerEngine/summer/tree/HEAD/library/skills/scene-scripting) · ★109 · 4 copies)
- **scene-scriptwriter**: 编写详细的场景剧本，定义每个场景的出场人物、对白/旁白、动作指示、镜头提示和情绪节奏。是连接角色设计与分镜生图的桥梁。 ([buda-ai/bunny-agent](https://github.com/buda-ai/bunny-agent/tree/HEAD/templates/videodrone-agent/.claude/skills/scene-scriptwriter) · ★29 · 2 copies)
- **scene-sensory**: 场景描写的目的是让读者"身临其境"——不只是看到画面，而是感受到温度、 听到声音、闻到气味。好的场景描写是五感的交响乐。 ([ayermac/novelos](https://github.com/ayermac/novelos/tree/HEAD/novel_factory/skills/knowledge/scene-sensory) · ★5)
- **scene-sequence-audit**: Use this skill to audit Bloomrot chapters for scene-sequence continuity errors: duplicate beats, knowledge-state violations (a character discovering something they already know), event repetition across chapters, and structural breaks in the accumulation of plot facts. Use when a scene beat may have been drafted twice, when a character's first encounter with something needs verification, or after a chapter is restructured. ([brianchristopherbrady/bloomrot](https://github.com/brianchristopherbrady/bloomrot/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/scene-sequence-audit))
- **scene-sequencing**: Structure scenes and control pacing using scene-sequel rhythm. Use when individual scenes work but don't accumulate, when pacing feels off (too rushed or too slow), when transitions feel mechanical, or when readers can follow but aren't compelled forward. Based on Dwight Swain's Goal-Conflict-Disaster and Reaction-Dilemma-Decision structure. ([jwynia/agent-skills](https://github.com/jwynia/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/creative/fiction/structure/scene-sequencing) · ★191 · 466 installs · 4 copies)
- **scene-sequencing**: 使用场景-续篇节奏来构建场景和控制节奏。适用于单个场景有效但缺乏累积效果、节奏感觉偏离（太快或太慢）、过渡感觉机械化、或读者能跟上但缺乏前进动力时。基于 Dwight Swain 的目标-冲突-灾难和反应-困境-决定结构。 ([alin776/ai_writer](https://github.com/alin776/ai_writer/tree/HEAD/backend/app/services/agent/skills/structure/scene-sequencing) · ★2)
- **scene-serialization-undo-multiedit**: Use for scene serialization, stable IDs, undo/redo, multi-edit, dirty state, Play enter/exit preservation. ([Aurum-Edmund/Fire-Engine](https://github.com/Aurum-Edmund/Fire-Engine/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/scene-serialization-undo-multiedit) · 2 copies)
- **scene-set**: Lock the geometry of a multi-shot AI video in one set file before any picture, then check every generated still against it - room, furniture, props, every person's seat and facing, one camera per setup. Use when planning shots or stills, writing image prompts for a scene, checking a still before paying for video, or when shots of one scene disagree (a moved table, swapped seats, flipped eyelines, wrong heights, light from the wrong side). ([Maksim-Burtsev/ai-film-crew](https://github.com/Maksim-Burtsev/ai-film-crew/tree/HEAD/skills/scene-set))

- **scene-sketch**: Use when a game idea must be shown as a picture - the `show-idea` slot while brainstorming in a Unity project, or the owner asks to sketch a moment, a scene or a sequence of play ("намалюй ідею", "покажи, як це буде"). - **scene-splitter**: Splits a plain English story into a numbered list of SCENES — each scene being one moment that gets exactly one illustration AND one narration clip downstream. The first step of the AI Storybook pipeline. Tuned for beginner-level stories (short sentences, ~8-12 scenes), with a per-scene length cap so each scene fits one phone screen without scrolling. Use this skill whenever the user wants to split a story into scenes, prepare a story for the storybook pipeline, break a story into pages/panels, or produce a scenes spine for the illustrator and narrator. Trigger on phrases like "split this sto… ([hassancs91/claude-image-generation](https://github.com/hassancs91/claude-image-generation/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/scene-splitter) · ★115)
- **scene-stack-demo-mode-coordination**: Fix infinite loops in demo/showcase modes for scene stack architectures. Use when: (1) Demo mode gets stuck repeating the same scene, (2) scene cycling doesn't progress through all scenes, (3) orchestrator scene's on_resume isn't being called, (4) pushed scenes bypass the demo controller. Applies to game engines, UI frameworks, and any stack-based state machine with automated testing or demo modes. ([strataga/claude-setup](https://github.com/strataga/claude-setup/tree/HEAD/skills/scene-stack-demo-mode-coordination))
- **scene-standard**: The Wildermyth standard every scene and conversation meets, as checkable rules: a scene exists only if it writes something back, roles are cast from the world, lines vary by the speaker's traits and tie to the player, narration never restates the screen, time jumps between scenes; and the first report a scene builder writes before any line. Use when writing, wiring, reviewing or grading a scene, talk, situation type, narration or journal line, in playtests, and at the start of any scene or story build. ([lamontaes/Political-Game-Git](https://github.com/lamontaes/Political-Game-Git/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/scene-standard) · 2 copies)
- **scene-state**: Слой «Wetter» — время суток, погода, сезон как параметры сцены (SceneState → Look) с плавной сменой по ключам; одинаково для 2D-картинок (цветокор, огни, небо), three (свет, небо, дымка, перекраска модели по группам) и Blender; реальная погода из Open-Meteo. Брать, когда у сцены есть настроение или его смена («свет погас», «сделай ночь», «пошёл снег»).
